# interest group stances on goods (tools/ig_stances.py); spec_igs_<group>_<good>: 1 favored, -1 opposed

# interest group scope: a timed approval modifier by its clout tier, X x1 or x2 (twice); none while Marginalized
spec_igs_approval = {
	if = {
		limit = { is_marginal = no }
		add_modifier = {
			name = $M$
			days = $DAYS$
			multiplier = spec_igs_tier_$X$
		}
	}
}

# country scope: the goods list's stance flags (ig_stances.mark)
spec_igs_mark = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				var:spec_igs_armed_forces_aeroplanes ?= 1
			}
		}
		set_variable = { name = spec_igs_fv_aeroplanes value = 1 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_variable = spec_igs_fv_aeroplanes }
		remove_variable = spec_igs_fv_aeroplanes
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				var:spec_igs_armed_forces_ammunition ?= 1

spec_igs_fav_landowners = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				AND = { spec_weight_grain >= 0.1 NOT = { has_variable = spec_igs_landowners_grain } }
				AND = { spec_weight_sugar >= 0.1 NOT = { has_variable = spec_igs_landowners_sugar } }
				AND = { spec_weight_tobacco >= 0.1 NOT = { has_variable = spec_igs_landowners_tobacco } }
				AND = { spec_weight_wine >= 0.1 NOT = { has_variable = spec_igs_landowners_wine } }
				AND = { spec_weight_tea >= 0.1 NOT = { has_variable = spec_igs_landowners_tea } }
				AND = { spec_weight_coffee >= 0.1 NOT = { has_variable = spec_igs_landowners_coffee } }
				AND = { spec_weight_silk >= 0.1 NOT = { has_variable = spec_igs_landowners_silk } }
				AND = { spec_weight_dye >= 0.1 NOT = { has_variable = spec_igs_landowners_dye } }
				AND = { spec_weight_meat >= 0.1 NOT = { has_variable = spec_igs_landowners_meat } }
			}
		}
		random_list = {
			3 = {
				trigger = { spec_weight_grain >= 0.1 NOT = { has_variable = spec_igs_landowners_grain } }
				set_variable = { name = spec_igs_landowners_grain value = 1 }
				if = {
					limit = { is_player = yes has_variable = spec_igs_rolled }
					if = {
						limit = { has_game_rule = spec_goods_news_detailed }

spec_igs_dis_landowners = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				AND = { spec_weight_steel >= 0.1 NOT = { has_variable = spec_igs_landowners_steel } }
				AND = { spec_weight_engines >= 0.1 NOT = { has_variable = spec_igs_landowners_engines } }
			}
		}
		random_list = {
			1 = {
				trigger = { spec_weight_steel >= 0.1 NOT = { has_variable = spec_igs_landowners_steel } }
				set_variable = { name = spec_igs_landowners_steel value = -1 }
				if = {
					limit = { is_player = yes has_variable = spec_igs_rolled }
					if = {
						limit = { has_game_rule = spec_goods_news_detailed }
						ig:ig_landowners = { save_scope_as = spec_note_ig }
						g:steel = { save_scope_as = spec_note_good }
						post_notification = spec_igs_dis_new
					}
					else = {
						spec_queue_market_news = { FIELD = opinions }
					}
				}
			}
			1 = {
				trigger = { spec_weight_engines >= 0.1 NOT = { has_variable = spec_igs_landowners_engines } }
				set_variable = { name = spec_igs_landowners_engines value = -1 }
				if = {
					limit = { is_player = yes has_variable = spec_igs_rolled }
					if = {
						limit = { has_game_rule = spec_goods_news_detailed }
						ig:ig_landowners = { save_scope_as = spec_note_ig }
						g:engines = { save_scope_as = spec_note_good }
						post_notification = spec_igs_dis_new
					}
					else = {
						spec_queue_market_news = { FIELD = opinions }
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

							}
							else = {
								spec_queue_market_news = { FIELD = opinions }
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
			random = {
				chance = 50
				spec_igs_dis_landowners = yes
			}
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { spec_igs_nf_landowners < 2 }
		spec_igs_fav_landowners = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = { spec_igs_nf_landowners < 2 }
		spec_igs_fav_landowners = yes
	}
	spec_igs_mark = yes
}

spec_igs_dis_industrialists = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				AND = { spec_weight_grain >= 0.1 NOT = { has_variable = spec_igs_industrialists_grain } }
			}
		}
		random_list = {
			1 = {
				trigger = { spec_weight_grain >= 0.1 NOT = { has_variable = spec_igs_industrialists_grain } }
				set_variable = { name = spec_igs_industrialists_grain value = -1 }
				if = {
					limit = { is_player = yes has_variable = spec_igs_rolled }
					if = {
						limit = { has_game_rule = spec_goods_news_detailed }
						ig:ig_industrialists = { save_scope_as = spec_note_ig }
						g:grain = { save_scope_as = spec_note_good }
						post_notification = spec_igs_dis_new
					}
					else = {
						spec_queue_market_news = { FIELD = opinions }
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

spec_igs_fav_armed_forces = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				AND = { spec_weight_small_arms >= 0.1 NOT = { has_variable = spec_igs_armed_forces_small_arms } }
				AND = { spec_weight_artillery >= 0.1 NOT = { has_variable = spec_igs_armed_forces_artillery } }
				AND = { spec_weight_ammunition >= 0.1 NOT = { has_variable = spec_igs_armed_forces_ammunition } }
				AND = { spec_weight_explosives >= 0.1 NOT = { has_variable = spec_igs_armed_forces_explosives } }
				AND = { spec_weight_tanks >= 0.1 NOT = { has_variable = spec_igs_armed_forces_tanks } }
				AND = { spec_weight_aeroplanes >= 0.1 NOT = { has_variable = spec_igs_armed_forces_aeroplanes } }
				AND = { spec_weight_lead >= 0.1 NOT = { has_variable = spec_igs_armed_forces_lead } }

						var:spec_igs_rural_folk_tools ?= -1

							else = {

							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
			random = {
				chance = 50
				spec_igs_dis_rural_folk = yes
			}
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { spec_igs_nf_rural_folk < 2 }
		spec_igs_fav_rural_folk = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = { spec_igs_nf_rural_folk < 2 }
		spec_igs_fav_rural_folk = yes
	}
	spec_igs_mark = yes
}

spec_igs_fav_intelligentsia = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				AND = { spec_weight_paper >= 0.1 NOT = { has_variable = spec_igs_intelligentsia_paper } }
				AND = { spec_weight_telephones >= 0.1 NOT = { has_variable = spec_igs_intelligentsia_telephones } }
				AND = { spec_weight_radios >= 0.1 NOT = { has_variable = spec_igs_intelligentsia_radios } }
				AND = { spec_weight_glass >= 0.1 NOT = { has_variable = spec_igs_intelligentsia_glass } }
				AND = { spec_weight_fine_art >= 0.1 NOT = { has_variable = spec_igs_intelligentsia_fine_art } }
				AND = { spec_weight_electricity >= 0.1 NOT = { has_variable = spec_igs_intelligentsia_electricity } }
			}
		}
		random_list = {
			3 = {
				trigger = { spec_weight_paper >= 0.1 NOT = { has_variable = spec_igs_intelligentsia_paper } }
				set_variable = { name = spec_igs_intelligentsia_paper value = 1 }
				if = {
					limit = { is_player = yes has_variable = spec_igs_rolled }
					if = {
						limit = { has_game_rule = spec_goods_news_detailed }
						ig:ig_intelligentsia = { save_scope_as = spec_note_ig }
						g:paper = { save_scope_as = spec_note_good }
						post_notification = spec_igs_fav_new
					}
					else = {
						spec_queue_market_news = { FIELD = opinions }
					}
				}
			}
			2 = {
				trigger = { spec_weight_telephones >= 0.1 NOT = { has_variable = spec_igs_intelligentsia_telephones } }
				set_variable = { name = spec_igs_intelligentsia_telephones value = 1 }
				if = {
					limit = { is_player = yes has_variable = spec_igs_rolled }
					if = {
						limit = { has_game_rule = spec_goods_news_detailed }

						spec_queue_market_news = { FIELD = opinions }

				}
			}
			1 = {
				trigger = { spec_weight_glass >= 0.1 NOT = { has_variable = spec_igs_intelligentsia_glass } }
				set_variable = { name = spec_igs_intelligentsia_glass value = 1 }
				if = {

					if = {
						limit = { has_game_rule = spec_goods_news_detailed }
						ig:ig_intelligentsia = { save_scope_as = spec_note_ig }

			1 = {

spec_igs_dis_intelligentsia = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				AND = { spec_weight_opium >= 0.1 NOT = { has_variable = spec_igs_intelligentsia_opium } }
				AND = { spec_weight_liquor >= 0.1 NOT = { has_variable = spec_igs_intelligentsia_liquor } }
			}
		}
		random_list = {
			3 = {
				trigger = { spec_weight_opium >= 0.1 NOT = { has_variable = spec_igs_intelligentsia_opium } }
				set_variable = { name = spec_igs_intelligentsia_opium value = -1 }
				if = {
					limit = { is_player = yes has_variable = spec_igs_rolled }
					if = {
						limit = { has_game_rule = spec_goods_news_detailed }
						ig:ig_intelligentsia = { save_scope_as = spec_note_ig }
						g:opium = { save_scope_as = spec_note_good }
						post_notification = spec_igs_dis_new
					}
					else = {
						spec_queue_market_news = { FIELD = opinions }
					}
				}
			}
			1 = {
				trigger = { spec_weight_liquor >= 0.1 NOT = { has_variable = spec_igs_intelligentsia_liquor } }
				set_variable = { name = spec_igs_intelligentsia_liquor value = -1 }
				if = {
					limit = { is_player = yes has_variable = spec_igs_rolled }
					if = {
						limit = { has_game_rule = spec_goods_news_detailed }
						ig:ig_intelligentsia = { save_scope_as = spec_note_ig }
						g:liquor = { save_scope_as = spec_note_good }
						post_notification = spec_igs_dis_new
					}

	}

	if = {

				AND = { spec_weight_clothes >= 0.1 NOT = { has_variable = spec_igs_trade_unions_clothes } }

				AND = { spec_weight_steel >= 0.1 NOT = { has_variable = spec_igs_trade_unions_steel } }
				AND = { spec_weight_furniture >= 0.1 NOT = { has_variable = spec_igs_trade_unions_furniture } }
				AND = { spec_weight_fabric >= 0.1 NOT = { has_variable = spec_igs_trade_unions_fabric } }
			}
		}
		random_list = {
			3 = {
				trigger = { spec_weight_groceries >= 0.1 NOT = { has_variable = spec_igs_trade_unions_groceries } }
				set_variable = { name = spec_igs_trade_unions_groceries value = 1 }

					limit = { is_player = yes has_variable = spec_igs_rolled }
					if = {
						limit = { has_game_rule = spec_goods_news_detailed }
						ig:ig_trade_unions = { save_scope_as = spec_note_ig }
						g:groceries = { save_scope_as = spec_note_good }
						post_notification = spec_igs_fav_new
					}
					else = {
						spec_queue_market_news = { FIELD = opinions }
					}
				}
			}
			3 = {
				trigger = { spec_weight_clothes >= 0.1 NOT = { has_variable = spec_igs_trade_unions_clothes } }
				set_variable = { name = spec_igs_trade_unions_clothes value = 1 }
				if = {
					limit = { is_player = yes has_variable = spec_igs_rolled }
					if = {
						limit = { has_game_rule = spec_goods_news_detailed }
						ig:ig_trade_unions = { save_scope_as = spec_note_ig }
						g:clothes = { save_scope_as = spec_note_good }
						post_notification = spec_igs_fav_new
					}
					else = {
						spec_queue_market_news = { FIELD = opinions }
					}
				}
			}
			1 = {
				trigger = { spec_weight_coal >= 0.1 NOT = { has_variable = spec_igs_trade_unions_coal } }
				set_variable = { name = spec_igs_trade_unions_coal value = 1 }
				if = {
					limit = { is_player = yes has_variable = spec_igs_rolled }
					if = {
						limit = { has_game_rule = spec_goods_news_detailed }
						ig:ig_trade_unions = { save_scope_as = spec_note_ig }
						g:coal = { save_scope_as = spec_note_good }
						post_notification = spec_igs_fav_new
					}
					else = {
						spec_queue_market_news = { FIELD = opinions }
					}
				}
			}
			1 = {
				trigger = { spec_weight_steel >= 0.1 NOT = { has_variable = spec_igs_trade_unions_steel } }
				set_variable = { name = spec_igs_trade_unions_steel value = 1 }
				if = {
					limit = { is_player = yes has_variable = spec_igs_rolled }
					if = {
						limit = { has_game_rule = spec_goods_news_detailed }
						ig:ig_trade_unions = { save_scope_as = spec_note_ig }
						g:steel = { save_scope_as = spec_note_good }
						post_notification = spec_igs_fav_new
					}
					else = {
						spec_queue_market_news = { FIELD = opinions }
					}
				}
			}
			1 = {
				trigger = { spec_weight_furniture >= 0.1 NOT = { has_variable = spec_igs_trade_unions_furniture } }
				set_variable = { name = spec_igs_trade_unions_furniture value = 1 }
				if = {
					limit = { is_player = yes has_variable = spec_igs_rolled }
					if = {
						limit = { has_game_rule = spec_goods_news_detailed }
						ig:ig_trade_unions = { save_scope_as = spec_note_ig }
						g:furniture = { save_scope_as = spec_note_good }
						post_notification = spec_igs_fav_new
					}
					else = {
						spec_queue_market_news = { FIELD = opinions }
					}
				}
			}
			1 = {
				trigger = { spec_weight_fabric >= 0.1 NOT = { has_variable = spec_igs_trade_unions_fabric } }
				set_variable = { name = spec_igs_trade_unions_fabric value = 1 }
				if = {
					limit = { is_player = yes has_variable = spec_igs_rolled }
					if = {
						limit = { has_game_rule = spec_goods_news_detailed }
						ig:ig_trade_unions = { save_scope_as = spec_note_ig }
						g:fabric = { save_scope_as = spec_note_good }
						post_notification = spec_igs_fav_new

			}
		}
	}

spec_igs_dis_trade_unions = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				AND = { spec_weight_liquor >= 0.1 NOT = { has_variable = spec_igs_trade_unions_liquor } }
				AND = { spec_weight_opium >= 0.1 NOT = { has_variable = spec_igs_trade_unions_opium } }
			}
		}
		random_list = {
			2 = {
				trigger = { spec_weight_liquor >= 0.1 NOT = { has_variable = spec_igs_trade_unions_liquor } }
				set_variable = { name = spec_igs_trade_unions_liquor value = -1 }
				if = {
					limit = { is_player = yes has_variable = spec_igs_rolled }
					if = {
						limit = { has_game_rule = spec_goods_news_detailed }
						ig:ig_trade_unions = { save_scope_as = spec_note_ig }
						g:liquor = { save_scope_as = spec_note_good }
						post_notification = spec_igs_dis_new
					}
					else = {
						spec_queue_market_news = { FIELD = opinions }
					}
				}
			}
			1 = {
				trigger = { spec_weight_opium >= 0.1 NOT = { has_variable = spec_igs_trade_unions_opium } }
				set_variable = { name = spec_igs_trade_unions_opium value = -1 }
				if = {
					limit = { is_player = yes has_variable = spec_igs_rolled }
					if = {
						limit = { has_game_rule = spec_goods_news_detailed }
						ig:ig_trade_unions = { save_scope_as = spec_note_ig }
						g:opium = { save_scope_as = spec_note_good }
						post_notification = spec_igs_dis_new
					}
					else = {
						spec_queue_market_news = { FIELD = opinions }
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

spec_igs_reroll_trade_unions = {
	random_list = {
		2 = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						var:spec_igs_trade_unions_groceries ?= 1
						var:spec_igs_trade_unions_clothes ?= 1
						var:spec_igs_trade_unions_coal ?= 1
						var:spec_igs_trade_unions_steel ?= 1
						var:spec_igs_trade_unions_furniture ?= 1
						var:spec_igs_trade_unions_fabric ?= 1
					}
				}
				random_list = {
					1 = {
						trigger = { var:spec_igs_trade_unions_groceries ?= 1 }
						remove_variable = spec_igs_trade_unions_groceries
						if = {
							limit = { is_player = yes has_variable = spec_igs_rolled }
							if = {
								limit = { has_game_rule = spec_goods_news_detailed }
								ig:ig_trade_unions = { save_scope_as = spec_note_ig }
								g:groceries = { save_scope_as = spec_note_good }
								post_notification = spec_igs_fav_lost
							}
							else = {
								spec_queue_market_news = { FIELD = opinions }
							}
						}
					}
					1 = {
						trigger = { var:spec_igs_trade_unions_clothes ?= 1 }
						remove_variable = spec_igs_trade_unions_clothes
						if = {
							limit = { is_player = yes has_variable = spec_igs_rolled }
							if = {
								limit = { has_game_rule = spec_goods_news_detailed }
								ig:ig_trade_unions = { save_scope_as = spec_note_ig }
								g:clothes = { save_scope_as = spec_note_good }
								post_notification = spec_igs_fav_lost
							}
							else = {
								spec_queue_market_news = { FIELD = opinions }
							}
						}
					}
					1 = {
						trigger = { var:spec_igs_trade_unions_coal ?= 1 }
						remove_variable = spec_igs_trade_unions_coal
						if = {
							limit = { is_player = yes has_variable = spec_igs_rolled }
							if = {
								limit = { has_game_rule = spec_goods_news_detailed }
								ig:ig_trade_unions = { save_scope_as = spec_note_ig }
								g:coal = { save_scope_as = spec_note_good }
								post_notification = spec_igs_fav_lost
							}
							else = {
								spec_queue_market_news = { FIELD = opinions }
							}
						}
					}
					1 = {
						trigger = { var:spec_igs_trade_unions_steel ?= 1 }
						remove_variable = spec_igs_trade_unions_steel
						if = {
							limit = { is_player = yes has_variable = spec_igs_rolled }
							if = {
								limit = { has_game_rule = spec_goods_news_detailed }
								ig:ig_trade_unions = { save_scope_as = spec_note_ig }
								g:steel = { save_scope_as = spec_note_good }
								post_notification = spec_igs_fav_lost
							}
							else = {
								spec_queue_market_news = { FIELD = opinions }
							}
						}
					}
					1 = {
						trigger = { var:spec_igs_trade_unions_furniture ?= 1 }
						remove_variable = spec_igs_trade_unions_furniture
						if = {
							limit = { is_player = yes has_variable = spec_igs_rolled }
							if = {
								limit = { has_game_rule = spec_goods_news_detailed }
								ig:ig_trade_unions = { save_scope_as = spec_note_ig }
								g:furniture = { save_scope_as = spec_note_good }
								post_notification = spec_igs_fav_lost
							}
							else = {
								spec_queue_market_news = { FIELD = opinions }
							}
						}
					}
					1 = {
						trigger = { var:spec_igs_trade_unions_fabric ?= 1 }
						remove_variable = spec_igs_trade_unions_fabric
						if = {
							limit = { is_player = yes has_variable = spec_igs_rolled }
							if = {
								limit = { has_game_rule = spec_goods_news_detailed }
								ig:ig_trade_unions = { save_scope_as = spec_note_ig }
								g:fabric = { save_scope_as = spec_note_good }
								post_notification = spec_igs_fav_lost
							}
							else = {
								spec_queue_market_news = { FIELD = opinions }
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		1 = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						var:spec_igs_trade_unions_liquor ?= -1
						var:spec_igs_trade_unions_opium ?= -1
					}
				}
				random_list = {
					1 = {
						trigger = { var:spec_igs_trade_unions_liquor ?= -1 }
						remove_variable = spec_igs_trade_unions_liquor
						if = {
							limit = { is_player = yes has_variable = spec_igs_rolled }
							if = {
								limit = { has_game_rule = spec_goods_news_detailed }
								ig:ig_trade_unions = { save_scope_as = spec_note_ig }
								g:liquor = { save_scope_as = spec_note_good }
								post_notification = spec_igs_dis_lost
							}
							else = {
								spec_queue_market_news = { FIELD = opinions }
							}
						}
					}
					1 = {
						trigger = { var:spec_igs_trade_unions_opium ?= -1 }
						remove_variable = spec_igs_trade_unions_opium
						if = {
							limit = { is_player = yes has_variable = spec_igs_rolled }
							if = {
								limit = { has_game_rule = spec_goods_news_detailed }
								ig:ig_trade_unions = { save_scope_as = spec_note_ig }
								g:opium = { save_scope_as = spec_note_good }
								post_notification = spec_igs_dis_lost
							}
							else = {
								spec_queue_market_news = { FIELD = opinions }
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
			random = {
				chance = 50
				spec_igs_dis_trade_unions = yes
			}
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { spec_igs_nf_trade_unions < 2 }
		spec_igs_fav_trade_unions = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = { spec_igs_nf_trade_unions < 2 }
		spec_igs_fav_trade_unions = yes
	}
	spec_igs_mark = yes
}

# each group's first stances, from the goods its market trades
spec_igs_roll = {
	spec_igs_fav_landowners = yes
	spec_igs_fav_landowners = yes
	random = {
		chance = 50
		spec_igs_dis_landowners = yes
	}
	spec_igs_fav_industrialists = yes
	spec_igs_fav_industrialists = yes
	random = {
		chance = 50
		spec_igs_dis_industrialists = yes
	}
	spec_igs_fav_armed_forces = yes
	spec_igs_fav_armed_forces = yes
	random = {
		chance = 50
		spec_igs_dis_armed_forces = yes
	}
	spec_igs_fav_devout = yes
	spec_igs_fav_devout = yes
	random = {
		chance = 50
		spec_igs_dis_devout = yes
	}
	spec_igs_fav_rural_folk = yes
	spec_igs_fav_rural_folk = yes
	random = {
		chance = 50
		spec_igs_dis_rural_folk = yes
	}
	spec_igs_fav_petty_bourgeoisie = yes
	spec_igs_fav_petty_bourgeoisie = yes
	random = {
		chance = 50
		spec_igs_dis_petty_bourgeoisie = yes
	}
	spec_igs_fav_intelligentsia = yes
	spec_igs_fav_intelligentsia = yes
	random = {
		chance = 50
		spec_igs_dis_intelligentsia = yes
	}
	spec_igs_fav_trade_unions = yes
	spec_igs_fav_trade_unions = yes
	random = {
		chance = 50
		spec_igs_dis_trade_unions = yes
	}
	spec_igs_mark = yes
	set_variable = { name = spec_igs_rolled value = 1 }
}

spec_igs_on_boom_aeroplanes = {
	if = {
		limit = { var:spec_igs_armed_forces_aeroplanes ?= 1 }
		ig:ig_armed_forces ?= { spec_igs_approval = { M = spec_igs_boom DAYS = 180 X = x1 } }
	}
}

spec_igs_on_bust_aeroplanes = {  # param CHANCE: the group may drop the good
	if = {
		limit = { var:spec_igs_armed_forces_aeroplanes ?= 1 }
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = spec_issue_aeroplanes }
			ig:ig_armed_forces ?= { remove_modifier = spec_igs_issue }
			ig:ig_armed_forces ?= { spec_igs_approval = { M = spec_igs_bust DAYS = 180 X = x2 } }
		}
		else = {
			ig:ig_armed_forces ?= { spec_igs_approval = { M = spec_igs_bust DAYS = 180 X = x1 } }
		}
		random = {
			chance = $CHANCE$
			remove_variable = spec_igs_armed_forces_aeroplanes
			if = {
				limit = { is_player = yes has_variable = spec_igs_rolled }
				if = {
					limit = { has_game_rule = spec_goods_news_detailed }
					ig:ig_armed_forces = { save_scope_as = spec_note_ig }
					g:aeroplanes = { save_scope_as = spec_note_good }
					post_notification = spec_igs_fav_lost
				}
				else = {
					spec_queue_market_news = { FIELD = opinions }
				}
			}
		}
	}
	spec_igs_mark = yes
}

			if = {

				if = {

					ig:ig_devout = { save_scope_as = spec_note_ig }
					g:opium = { save_scope_as = spec_note_good }
					post_notification = spec_igs_fav_lost

			limit = { has_variable = spec_issue_tanks }
			ig:ig_armed_forces ?= { remove_modifier = spec_igs_issue }
			ig:ig_armed_forces ?= { spec_igs_approval = { M = spec_igs_bust DAYS = 180 X = x2 } }
		}
		else = {
			ig:ig_armed_forces ?= { spec_igs_approval = { M = spec_igs_bust DAYS = 180 X = x1 } }
		}
		random = {
			chance = $CHANCE$
			remove_variable = spec_igs_armed_forces_tanks
			if = {
				limit = { is_player = yes has_variable = spec_igs_rolled }
				if = {
					limit = { has_game_rule = spec_goods_news_detailed }
					ig:ig_armed_forces = { save_scope_as = spec_note_ig }
					g:tanks = { save_scope_as = spec_note_good }
					post_notification = spec_igs_fav_lost
				}
				else = {
					spec_queue_market_news = { FIELD = opinions }
				}
			}
		}
	}
	spec_igs_mark = yes
}
